				mux IMUX_LSR[1] = TIE1 | X0_W[0] | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_E[1] | X2_W1[3] | X2_W2[3] | X2_E1[3] | X2_E2[3] | X2_S1[3] | X2_S2[3] | X2_N1[3] | X2_N2[3] | PCLK[0] | PCLK[1] | PCLK[2] | PCLK[3] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];
				mux IMUX_CE[0] = TIE1 | X0_W[1] | X0_E[0] | X0_S[1] | X0_N[0] | X2_W1[7] | X2_W2[7] | X2_E1[7] | X2_E2[7] | X2_S1[7] | X2_S2[7] | X2_N1[7] | X2_N2[7] | PCLK[0] | PCLK[1] | PCLK[2] | PCLK[3] | PCLK[4] | PCLK[5] | PCLK[6] | PCLK[7];

			}

		bel_slot BEL_INT: routing;

			cell CELL;

			bel SLICE[0] {
				input A[0] = IMUX_A[0];

			bel SLICE[1] {
				input A[0] = IMUX_A[2];

				mux IMUX_DDRDLL_CLK = IMUX_CLK[1] | ECLK_TO_DDRDLL[0] | ECLK_TO_DDRDLL[1] | ECLK_TO_DDRDLL[2] | ECLK_TO_DDRDLL[3];
			}

				input RST = IMUX_LSR[1];
				input FREEZE = IMUX_MUXC[2];
				input CLKIN = IMUX_DDRDLL_CLK;
				input UDDCNTL_N = IMUX_MUXD[1];
				output LOCK = OUT_Q[2];
				output DCNTL[0] = OUT_F[0];
				output DCNTL[1] = OUT_F[1];
				output DCNTL[2] = OUT_F[2];

				output DCNTL[8] = OUT_Q[0];
				output DIVOSCIB = OUT_Q[1];
			}
